HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000318/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_169ef893040f1382dedf","text":"4. **Wealth as imagined sufficiency.** Evidence: `ح س ب B003` (sufficiency/enoughness) with `م و ل B001`, activated by the mercy frame of 104:0 and divine fire in 104:6. Structural cue: the possessive ماله places wealth as his supposed sufficient resource. Assigned role: حسب supplies the \"enough for me\" claim beneath the thought. Abductive move: possession is mistaken for what suffices before God and society. Reading change: the ayah exposes self-sufficiency as the root of the immortalizing fantasy.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000318/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_440308944c785930ebe3","text":"2. **Wealth imagined as sufficient against change.** Fixed `يَحْسَبُ` also activates `ح س ب B003` (sufficiency and enoughness) in proximity to fixed wealth, `م و ل B001`, and fixed permanence, `خ ل د B001`. `104:4`-`104:6` then supply casting, breaking, and kindling, all processes that wealth fails to stop. The packet supplies the claimed cause and its material defeat; I infer that the suppressed premise is \"my wealth is enough.\" The reading changes from a mistaken forecast to a claim of self-sufficiency against transformation and judgment.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000318/B002","root_000429/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_46f9b8dd8f95250c7a60","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:3:1\",\"root_000318\",[\"B002\"]],[\"104:3:4\",\"root_000429\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Kalıcılık düşüncesi geçici bir yanılgı değil, yüreğe yerleşmiş ve sonraki ateşin açığa çıkardığı iç yönelim hâline gelir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000318/B001","root_000318/B002","root_000429/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_4b6a14c9e9656275ac56","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:3:1\",\"root_000318\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]],[\"104:3:4\",\"root_000429\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Sayısal hesap, sınırlı miktarı sınırsız süreye kanıt sayan bir sanıya dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000429/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_4cf16703cfc767c1444c","text":"2. **Clinging to wealth becomes sealed permanence.** Evidence: `خ ل د B002` (leaning, clinging, staying close), activated by `و ص د B001` in 104:8 and `م د د B004` in 104:9. Structural cue: the imagined \"made him endure\" is later answered by a shut, extended condition. Assigned role: خلود supplies both desired permanence and adhesive attachment. Abductive move: punishment converts chosen clinging into inescapable clinging imposed on him. Reading change: the error is not abstract belief in immortality only; it is a posture of sticking to wealth that is reversed into confinement.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_4d023f8a620359259548","text":"104:7 caused the sharpest change: يحسب is not left in the head but is relocated into the فؤاد as an inward heat/kindling. 104:8-9 then turned \"أخلده\" into irony: the permanence he imagined appears, but as sealed duration and extension rather than life-giving endurance.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000429/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_60b84732efacbe7a7d4a","text":"3. **The false thought lodged in the heart.** Evidence: `خ ل د B004` (settled thought in the heart) and `ح س ب B002`, activated by `ف ء د B002` and `ط ل ع B003` in 104:7. Structural cue: a mental verb in 104:3 is later met by fire that reaches the أفئدة. Assigned role: خلد names the inner settling-place of the delusion. Abductive move: if the fire reaches the heart, it reaches the place where false حساب has settled. Reading change: 104:3 becomes the interior target of the later fire, not merely a report of wrong opinion.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000318/B003","root_000429/B001","root_001457/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_98cd6ac178ffd987f82c","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:3:1\",\"root_000318\",[\"B003\"]],[\"104:3:3\",\"root_001457\",[\"B001\"]],[\"104:3:4\",\"root_000429\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Servete, değişim ve hüküm karşısında tek başına yeterli olma gücü yakıştırılır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001122/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_a0a78bd804b4c6554af8","text":"`104:7` was the decisive lexical surprise: `ف ء د B002` made `خ ل د B004`, settled thought in the heart, directly functional rather than merely associative. `104:8` then corrected a purely temporal reading of permanence by adding sealed spatial arrest. Together they preserve two distinct activations of the fixed causative: wealth settles a permanence-belief inwardly, and chosen clinging is reversed into imposed staying.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000318/B001","root_000989/B001","root_001457/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_baa4e75aa3e554a50382","text":"1. **Calculation collapsing into supposition.** Fixed `يَحْسَبُ` holds together `ح س ب B001` (counting and reckoning) and `ح س ب B002` (mental supposition); fixed `مَالَهُ` is `م و ل B001`, and fixed `أَخْلَدَهُ` activates `خ ل د B001` (making something endure). The immediately preceding `عَدَّدَهُ`, `ع د د B001`, supplies the numerical input. The packet gives counting followed by supposition; I infer that the former licenses the latter. The ayah now diagnoses a category error in which a finite count is treated as proof of unbounded duration.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000429/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_bfe283a28d1b8008624e","text":"3. **Permanence as an inwardly settled thought.** Fixed `أَخْلَدَهُ` activates `خ ل د B004`, a thought settled in the heart, when `104:7` names the inner-kindling heart through `ف ء د B002` and says the fire rises upon it through `ط ل ع B003` (overlooking and disclosing an interior). The packet supplies the belief and the later heart-directed disclosure; I infer that what the fire exposes is the permanence-calculus lodged inwardly. The ayah consequently reads not only as an erroneous proposition but as a settled interior orientation that the later fire reaches.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000429/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_d13ee748c19a43236620","text":"4. **A reversal from chosen clinging to imposed staying.** Fixed `أَخْلَدَهُ` further activates `خ ل د B002` (clinging, settling, and remaining close). `104:4` casts the subject away, while `104:8` seals the fire over them with `و ص د B001`. Those spatial facts are supplied; I infer the reversal linking them: attachment to wealth does not secure free continuity but culminates in immobilized continuity inside closure. The causative phrase can therefore read as wealth training its owner to cling, a habit whose promised permanence returns as confinement.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000318/B001","root_000989/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_d36d390aaf63d4dc9f39","text":"1. **Accounting becomes false permanence.** Evidence: `ح س ب B001` (counting/reckoning), `ح س ب B002` (supposition), `م و ل B001`, and `خ ل د B001` (enduring permanence); activated backward by `ع د د B001` in 104:2. Structural cue: enumeration immediately produces a mental claim about دوام. Assigned role: حساب is the bridge from arithmetic to belief. Abductive move: repeated counting trains the self to experience quantity as ontological security. Reading change: the ayah shows how the thought of wealth-made permanence is manufactured by counting.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000429/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f8bbb8f730a1898cdb02","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:3:4\",\"root_000429\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Servete isteyerek yapışma, sonunda çıkışı olmayan bir yerde zorla kalmaya dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
